Modeling - Chapter 219

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the modeling space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 209 and the convergence rate is O(733/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the modeling space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 209 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 733. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Every optimality condition deformation of the modeling object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 768. The moduli space has dimension at most 898 with 5 connected components.
